066 - equations MathPix is sure of, whose own render disagrees with the scan
==============================================================================

WHAT THIS LIST IS
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   No author LaTeX is used anywhere in this measurement. The ink distance
   compares MathPix's own `latex`, rendered standalone, against the scan
   crop of the same page region. Both sides derive from the document being
   read - so the list exists for ANY OCR'd document, with no gold of any
   kind. That is the point: it reaches the 99% of the corpus where no
   e-print will ever exist, which is exactly where the SLT route is blind.

   Selection: ink >= 6 AND confidence >= 0.9 AND not demoted.
   6 is the consumer's measured noise floor (p95 of the same expressions
   through two rasterizers). 0.9 keeps only rows MathPix is sure about, so
   every hit is a case where the OCR's own certainty and the page disagree.

POPULATION
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   49 documents, 4,342 equations.
      3392  measured (both artefacts present, conf >= 0.9, not demoted)
       898  skipped - confidence < 0.9
        47  skipped - demoted (the Rendered cell is a placeholder string,
            so measuring it compares '(not rendered)' with mathematics)
         4  skipped - no confidence value
         1  skipped - no standalone render

   HITS: 725 of 3392 measured = 21.4%

   That share is the headline. One equation in five that MathPix is
   confident about renders differently from the page it came from.

SORT

   out/067.txt inspects the top 10 of this list by eye. The short version:
   none of the ten is a content error - nine are typography (delimiter
   auto-sizing, \frac vs \dfrac, line and column spacing) and one is a
   grey scan background defeating the mask. 63% of these 725 rows are
   dominated by the layout features (stacked/centred/offset) rather than
   the symbol features (components/holes); ranking by the symbol half is
   the change that would surface content errors instead.
